> [!embed-item]- Demonomicon of Iggwilv
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Demonomicon of Iggwilv

</div>



# Demonomicon of Iggwilv
*Wondrous item, artifact (requires attunement)*  



This treatise, composed by "Iggwilv" the archmage, documents the Abyss's layers and inhabitants and is widely regarded as the most thorough and blasphemous tome of demonology in the multiverse. The tome recounts both the oldest and most current profanities of the Abyss and demons. Demons have attempted to censor the text, and while sections have been ripped from the book's spine, the general chapters remain, ever revealing demonic secrets. Caged behind lines of script roils a secret piece of the Abyss itself, which keeps the book up-to-date, no matter how many pages are removed, and it longs to be more than mere reference material.

## Abyssal Lore

You can reference the Demonomicon whenever you make an Intelligence check to discern information about demons or a Wisdom ([[Rules/Source/skills#Survival\|Survival]]) check related to the Abyss. When you do so, you gain [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/advantage-xphb\|Advantage]] on the check.

## Containment

The first ten pages of the Demonomicon are blank. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action while holding the book, you can target a Fiend that you can see that is trapped within the area of a [[Rules/Source/spells/magic-circle-xphb\|Magic Circle]] spell. The Fiend must succeed on a DC 20 Charisma saving throw with [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/disadvantage-xphb\|Disadvantage]] or become trapped within one of the Demonomicon's blank pages, which fills with writing detailing the trapped creature's widely known name and depravities. Once used, this action can't be used again until the next dawn.

When you finish a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/long-rest-xphb\|Long Rest]], if you and the Demonomicon are on the same plane of existence, one trapped creature within the book can attempt to possess you. You make a DC 20 Charisma saving throw. On a failed save, you are possessed by the creature, which controls you like a puppet. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, the possessing creature can release you and appear in the closest unoccupied space to you. On a successful save, the Fiend can't try to possess you again for 7 days (but another Fiend trapped in the book can certainly try).

When the tome is discovered, it has `1d4` Fiends occupying its pages—typically an assortment of demons.

## Ensnarement

While carrying the book, whenever you cast [[Rules/Source/spells/magic-circle-xphb\|Magic Circle]] naming only Fiends or cast [[Rules/Source/spells/planar-binding-xphb\|Planar Binding]] targeting a Fiend, the spell is cast at level 9, regardless of what level spell slot you used, if any. Additionally, the Fiend has [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/disadvantage-xphb\|Disadvantage]] on its saving throw against the spell.

## Fiendish Scourging

While carrying the book, when you make a damage roll for a spell you cast against a Fiend, you use the maximum possible result instead of rolling.

## Random Properties

The Artifact has the following random properties:

- 2 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-beneficial-properties-xdmg\|minor beneficial]] properties  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-detrimental-properties-xdmg\|minor detrimental]] property  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-major-detrimental-properties-xdmg\|major detrimental]] property  

## Spells

The book has 8 charges and regains `1d8` expended charges daily at dawn. While holding the book, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to cast one of the spells (save DC 20) on the following table. The table indicates how many charges you must expend to cast the spell.

| Spell | Charge Cost |
|-------|-------------|
| [[Rules/Source/spells/magic-circle-xphb\|Magic Circle]] | 1 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/magic-jar-xphb\|Magic Jar]] | 3 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/planar-ally-xphb\|Planar Ally]] | 3 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/planar-binding-xphb\|Planar Binding]] | 2 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/plane-shift-xphb\|Plane Shift]] (to the Abyss only) | 3 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/summon-fiend-xphb\|Summon Fiend]] | 3 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/tashas-hideous-laughter-xphb\|Tasha's Hideous Laughter]] | 0 |
## Destroying the Demonomicon

To destroy the book, six different demon lords must each tear out a sixth of the book's pages. If this occurs, the pages reappear after 24 hours. Before all those hours pass, anyone who opens the book's remaining binding is transported to a nascent layer of the Abyss that lies hidden within the book. At the heart of this deadly, semi-sentient domain lies a long-lost Artifact, Fraz-Urb'luu's Staff. If the staff is dragged from the pocket plane, the tome is reduced to a mundane and out-of-date copy of the Tome of Zyx, the work that served as the foundation of the Demonomicon of Iggwilv. The Tome of Zyx can be destroyed like any ordinary book. Once the staff emerges, the demon lord Fraz-Urb'luu knows instantly.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 252*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Dimensional Shackles

<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Dimensional Shackles

</div>



# Dimensional Shackles
*Wondrous item, rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/dimensional-shackles.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/dimensional-shackles.webp)


You can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to place these shackles on a creature that has the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Incapacitated\|Incapacitated]] condition. The shackles adjust to fit a creature of Small to Large size. The shackles prevent a creature bound by them from using any method of extradimensional movement, including teleportation or travel to a different plane of existence. They don't prevent the creature from passing through an interdimensional portal.

You and any creature you designate when you use the shackles can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to remove them. Once every 30 days, the bound creature can make a DC 30 Strength ([[Rules/Source/skills#Athletics\|Athletics]]) check. On a successful check, the creature breaks free and destroys the shackles.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 254. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

> [!embed-item]- Driftglobe
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Driftglobe

</div>



# Driftglobe
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/driftglobe.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/driftglobe.webp)

- **Weight**: 1.0 lbs.

This small sphere of thick glass weighs 1 pound. If you are within 60 feet of it, you can command it to emanate light equivalent to that of the [[Rules/Source/spells/light-xphb\|Light]] or [[Rules/Source/spells/daylight-xphb\|Daylight]] spell (your choice). Once used, the Daylight effect can't be used again until the next dawn.

You can issue another command as a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to make the illuminated globe rise into the air and float no more than 5 feet off the ground. The globe hovers in this way until you or another creature grasps it. If you move more than 60 feet from the hovering globe, it follows you until it is within 60 feet of you. It takes the shortest route to do so. If prevented from moving, the globe sinks gently to the ground and becomes inactive, and its light winks out.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 254*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Dust of Disappearance
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Dust of Disappearance

</div>



# Dust of Disappearance
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/dust-of-disappearance.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/dust-of-disappearance.webp)


This powder resembles fine sand. There is enough of it for one use. When you take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to throw the dust into the air, you and each creature and object within a 10-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from you have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Invisible\|Invisible]] condition for `2d4` minutes. The duration is the same for all subjects, and the dust is consumed when its magic takes effect. Immediately after an affected creature makes an attack roll, deals damage, or casts a spell, the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Invisible\|Invisible]] condition ends for that creature.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 255. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Dust of Dryness
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Dust of Dryness

</div>



# Dust of Dryness
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  



This small packet contains `1d6 + 4` pinches of dust. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action, you can sprinkle a pinch of the dust over water, turning up to a 15-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cube-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cube]] of water into one marble-sized pellet, which floats or rests near where the dust was sprinkled. The pellet's weight is negligible. A creature can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to smash the pellet against a hard surface, causing the pellet to shatter and release the water the dust absorbed. Doing so destroys the pellet and ends its magic.

As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action, you can sprinkle a pinch of the dust on an Elemental within 5 feet of yourself that is composed mostly of water (such as a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/elemental/water-elemental-xmm\|Water Elemental]] or a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/elemental/water-weird-xmm\|Water Weird]]). Such a creature exposed to a pinch of the dust makes a DC 13 Constitution saving throw, taking `10d6` Necrotic damage on a failed save or half as much damage on a successful one.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 255. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Dust of Sneezing and Choking
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Dust of Sneezing and Choking

</div>



# Dust of Sneezing and Choking
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/dust-of-sneezing-and-choking.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/dust-of-sneezing-and-choking.webp)


Found in a small container, this powder resembles [[Rules/Source/items/dust-of-disappearance-xdmg\|Dust of Disappearance]], and [[Rules/Source/spells/identify-xphb\|Identify]] reveals it to be such. There is enough of it for one use.

As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action, you can throw the dust into the air, forcing yourself and every creature in a 30-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from you to make a DC 15 Constitution saving throw. Constructs, Elementals, Oozes, Plants, and Undead succeed on the save automatically.

On a failed save, a creature begins sneezing uncontrollably; it has the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Incapacitated\|Incapacitated]] condition and is suffocating. The creature repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. The effect also ends on any creature targeted by a [[Rules/Source/spells/lesser-restoration-xphb\|Lesser Restoration]] spell.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 255. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

# Dwarven Plate
*Very rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/dwarven-plate.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/dwarven-plate.webp)


While wearing this armor, you gain a +2 bonus to [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/armor-class-xphb\|Armor Class]]. In addition, if an effect moves you against your will along the ground, you can take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/reaction-xphb\|Reaction]] to reduce the distance you are moved by up to 10 feet.

**Variants**:
- [Dwarven Half Plate Armor](#Dwarven%20Half%20Plate%20Armor)
- [Dwarven Plate Armor](#Dwarven%20Plate%20Armor)

### Dwarven Half Plate Armor

- **Armor Class**: 15 + Dex modifier (max of +2)
- **Stealth**: The wearer has disadvantage on Stealth (DEX) checks.
- **Weight**: 40.0 lbs.

### Dwarven Plate Armor

- **Armor Class**: 18
- **Strength**: Requires 15 STR.
- **Stealth**: The wearer has disadvantage on Stealth (DEX) checks.
- **Weight**: 65.0 lbs.


*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 255. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Dwarven Thrower
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Dwarven Thrower

# Dwarven Thrower
*Weapon ([[Rules/Source/items/warhammer-xphb\|warhammer]]), very rare (requires attunement by a Dwarf or a Creature Attuned to a [[Rules/Source/items/belt-of-dwarvenkind-xdmg\|Belt of Dwarvenkind]])*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/dwarven-thrower.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/dwarven-thrower.webp)

- **Damage**:
  - One-handed: 1d8 bludgeoning
  - Two-handed: 1d10 bludgeoning
- **Range**: 20/60
- **Properties**: [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Thrown\|Thrown]], [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Versatile\|Versatile]]
- **Weight**: 5.0 lbs.

You gain a +3 bonus to attack rolls and damage rolls made with this magic weapon. It has [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Thrown\|Thrown]] with a normal range of 20 feet and a long range of 60 feet. When you hit with a ranged attack using this weapon, it deals an extra `1d8` Force damage, or an extra `2d8` Force damage if the target is a Giant. Immediately after hitting or missing, the weapon flies back to your hand.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 256. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Orb of Dragonkind
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Orb of Dragonkind

</div>



# Orb of Dragonkind
*Wondrous item, artifact (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/orb-of-dragonkind.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/orb-of-dragonkind.webp)

- **Weight**: 3.0 lbs.

Long ago, in the Dragonlance setting, elves and humans waged a terrible war against chromatic dragons. When the world seemed doomed, the wizards of the Towers of High Sorcery came together and forged five Orbs of Dragonkind to help defeat the dragons. One orb was taken to each of the five towers, and there they were used to speed the war toward a victorious end. The wizards used the orbs to lure dragons to them, then destroyed the dragons with powerful magic.

As the Towers of High Sorcery fell in later ages, the orbs were destroyed or faded into legend, and only three are thought to survive. Their magic has been warped over the centuries. Their primary purpose of calling dragons still functions, but they also allow some measure of control over dragons.

Each orb contains the essence of an evil dragon, a presence that resents any attempt to coax magic from it. Those who try to wield an orb's magic but lack sufficient force of personality might find themselves under the orb's control.

An orb is an etched crystal globe about 10 inches in diameter. When used, it grows to about 20 inches in diameter, and mist swirls inside it.

While attuned to an orb, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to peer into the orb's depths. You must then make a DC 15 Charisma saving throw. On a successful save, you control the orb for as long as you remain attuned to it. On a failed save, the orb imposes the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Charmed\|Charmed]] condition on you for as long as you remain attuned to it.

While you are [[Rules/Source/conditions#Charmed\|Charmed]] by the orb, you can't voluntarily end your [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/attunement-xphb\|Attunement]] to it, and the orb casts [[Rules/Source/spells/suggestion-xphb\|Suggestion]] on you at will (save DC 18), urging you to work toward the evil ends it desires. The dragon essence within the orb might want many things: the annihilation of a particular society or organization, freedom from the orb, to spread suffering in the world, to advance the worship of "Tiamat", or something else the DM decides.

## Random Properties

An Orb of Dragonkind has the following random properties:

- 2 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-beneficial-properties-xdmg\|minor beneficial]] properties  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-detrimental-properties-xdmg\|minor detrimental]] property  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-major-detrimental-properties-xdmg\|major detrimental]] property  

## Spells

The orb has 7 charges and regains `1d4 + 3` expended charges daily at dawn. If you control the orb, you can cast one of the spells on the following table from it. The table indicates how many charges you must expend to cast the spell.

| Spell | Charge Cost |
|-------|-------------|
| [[Rules/Source/spells/cure-wounds-xphb\|Cure Wounds]] (level 9 version) | 4 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/daylight-xphb\|Daylight]] | 1 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/death-ward-xphb\|Death Ward]] | 2 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/detect-magic-xphb\|Detect Magic]] | 0 |
| [[Rules/Source/spells/scrying-xphb\|Scrying]] (save DC 18) | 3 |

## Call Dragons

While you control the orb, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to cause the orb to issue a telepathic call that extends in all directions for 40 miles. Chromatic dragons in range feel compelled to come to the orb as soon as possible by the most direct route. Dragon deities such as "Tiamat" are unaffected by this call. Chromatic dragons drawn to the orb might be [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hostile-attitude-xphb\|Hostile]] toward you for compelling them against their will. Once you have used this property, it can't be used again for 1 hour.

## Destroying an Orb

An Orb of Dragonkind has AC 20 and is destroyed if it takes damage from a +3 Weapon or a [[Rules/Source/spells/disintegrate-xphb\|Disintegrate]] spell. Nothing else can harm it.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 283. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> (as) and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


### E

> [!embed-item]- Bowl of Commanding Water Elementals

<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Bowl of Commanding Water Elementals

</div>



# Bowl of Commanding Water Elementals
*Wondrous item, rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/bowl-of-commanding-water-elementals.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/bowl-of-commanding-water-elementals.webp)


While this bowl is filled with water and you are within 5 feet of it, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to summon a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/elemental/water-elemental-xmm\|Water Elemental]]. The elemental appears in an unoccupied space as close to the bowl as possible, understands your languages, obeys your commands, and takes its turn immediately after you on your [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/initiative-xphb\|Initiative]] count. The elemental disappears after 1 hour, when it dies, or when you dismiss it as a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]]. The bowl can't be used this way again until the next dawn.

The bowl is about 1 foot in diameter and half as deep. It holds about 3 gallons.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 240. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

> [!embed-item]- Energy Bow
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Energy Bow

</div>



# Energy Bow
*Very rare (requires attunement)*  



You gain a +1 bonus to attack rolls and damage rolls made with this magic weapon, which has no string. Each time you pull your arm back in a firing motion, a magical arrow made of golden energy appears nocked and ready to fire. An arrow produced by this weapon deals Force damage instead of Piercing damage on a hit, and it disappears after it hits or misses its target. Until it disappears, the arrow emits [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bright-light-xphb\|Bright Light]] in a 20-foot radius and [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]] for an additional 20 feet.

This weapon has the following additional properties.

## Arrow of Restraint

Whenever you use this weapon to make a ranged attack against a creature, you can try to restrain the target instead of dealing damage to it. If the arrow hits, the target must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Restrained\|Restrained]] condition for 1 minute. As an action, a creature [[Rules/Source/conditions#Restrained\|Restrained]] by an arrow can make a DC 20 Strength ([[Rules/Source/skills#Athletics\|Athletics]]) check to try to break the restraint, ending the effect on itself on a successful check.

## Arrow of Transport

As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can fire one energy arrow from this weapon at a target you can see within 60 feet of yourself. The target can be either a willing Medium or smaller creature or an object that isn't being worn or carried, provided the object is small enough to fit inside a 5-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cube-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cube]]. The arrow teleports the target to an unoccupied space you can see within 10 feet of you.

## Energy Ladder

As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can loose a flurry of energy arrows from this weapon at a wall up to 60 feet away from yourself. The arrows become glowing rungs that stick out of the wall, forming a magical ladder up to 60 feet long on the wall. This ladder lasts for 1 minute before disappearing.

**Variants**:
- [Energy Longbow](#Energy%20Longbow)
- [Energy Shortbow](#Energy%20Shortbow)

### Energy Longbow

- **Damage**: 1d8 piercing
- **Range**: 150/600
- **Properties**: [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Ammunition\|Ammunition]], [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Heavy\|Heavy]], [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Two-Handed\|Two-Handed]]
- **Weight**: 2.0 lbs.

### Energy Shortbow

- **Damage**: 1d6 piercing
- **Range**: 80/320
- **Properties**: [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Ammunition\|Ammunition]], [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Two-Handed\|Two-Handed]]
- **Weight**: 2.0 lbs.


*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 257. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

# Eversmoking Bottle
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/eversmoking-bottle.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/eversmoking-bottle.webp)


As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can open or close this bottle.

Opening the bottle causes thick smoke to billow out, forming a cloud that fills a 60-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from the bottle. The area within the smoke is [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/heavily-obscured-xphb\|Heavily Obscured]].

Closing the bottle causes the cloud to become fixed in place until it disperses after 10 minutes. A strong wind (such as that created by the [[Rules/Source/spells/gust-of-wind-xphb\|Gust of Wind]] spell) disperses the cloud after 1 minute.

> [!embed-item]- Eye and Hand of Vecna
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Eye and Hand of Vecna

</div>



# Eye and Hand of Vecna
*Wondrous item, artifact (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/eye-and-hand-of-vecna.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/eye-and-hand-of-vecna.webp)


"Vecna" was a mighty wizard who, through magic and conquest, forged a terrible empire. For all his power, however, Vecna feared death and took steps to prevent his demise by becoming a lich.

A treacherous lieutenant named Kas brought Vecna's rule to an end in a terrible battle. Of Vecna, all that remained were one hand and one eye, grisly Artifacts that still seek to work Vecna's will in the world.

The [[Rules/Source/items/eye-of-vecna-xdmg\|Eye of Vecna]] and the [[Rules/Source/items/hand-of-vecna-xdmg\|Hand of Vecna]] are separate Artifacts that might be found together or separately. The eye looks like a bloodshot organ torn free from the socket. The hand is a shriveled left extremity.

## Random Properties of the Eye and Hand

The [[Rules/Source/items/eye-of-vecna-xdmg\|Eye of Vecna]] and the [[Rules/Source/items/hand-of-vecna-xdmg\|Hand of Vecna]] each have the following random properties

- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-beneficial-properties-xdmg\|minor beneficial]] property  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-major-beneficial-properties-xdmg\|major beneficial]] property  
- 1 [[Rules/Source/tables/artifact-properties-minor-detrimental-properties-xdmg\|minor detrimental]] property  

## Attuning to the Eye

To attune to the eye, you must press it into your empty socket. The eye grafts itself to your head and remains there until you die. If the eye is ever removed, you die.

## Properties of the Eye

While you are attuned to the eye, your alignment is Neutral Evil, and you gain the following benefits:

- **Truesight.** You have [[Rules/Source/senses#Truesight\|Truesight]] out to 240 feet.  
- **Spellcasting.** The eye has 8 charges and regains `1d4 + 4` expended charges daily at dawn. You can cast a spell on the [[Rules/Source/items/eye-of-vecna-xdmg\|Eye of Vecna]] Spells table from the eye (save DC 18). The table indicates how many charges you must expend to cast the spell. Each time you cast a spell from the eye, there is a 5 percent chance that Vecna tears your soul from your body, devours it, and then takes control of the body like a puppet. If that happens, you become an NPC under the DM's control.  

    **Eye of Vecna Spells**  

    | Spell | Charge Cost |  
    |-------|-------------|  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/clairvoyance-xphb\|Clairvoyance]] | 2 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/crown-of-madness-xphb\|Crown of Madness]] | 1 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/disintegrate-xphb\|Disintegrate]] | 4 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/dominate-monster-xphb\|Dominate Monster]] | 5 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/eyebite-xphb\|Eyebite]] | 4 |  
    ^eye-of-vecna-spells  
- **X-ray Vision.** You can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to gain X-ray vision with a range of 30 feet for 1 minute. To you, solid objects within that radius appear transparent and don't prevent light from passing through themselves. The vision can penetrate 1 foot of stone, 1 inch of common metal, or up to 3 feet of wood or dirt. Thicker substances block the vision, as does a thin sheet of lead.  

## Attuning to the Hand

To attune to the hand, you must press it against the stump where your left hand was. The hand grafts itself to your arm and becomes a functioning appendage. If the hand is ever removed, you die.

## Properties of the Hand

When you are attuned to the hand, your alignment is Neutral Evil, and you gain the following benefits:

- **Great Strength.** Your Strength becomes 20 unless it is already 20 or higher.  
- **Icy Touch.** Any melee spell attack you make with the hand and any melee attack made with a weapon held by it deals an extra `2d8` Cold damage on a hit.  
- **Spellcasting.** The hand has 8 charges and regains `1d4 + 4` expended charges daily at dawn. You can cast a spell on the [[Rules/Source/items/hand-of-vecna-xdmg\|Hand of Vecna]] Spells table from the hand (save DC 18). The table indicates how many charges you must expend to cast the spell. Each time you cast a spell from it, the hand casts [[Rules/Source/spells/suggestion-xphb\|Suggestion]] on you (save DC 18; no [[Rules/Source/conditions#Concentration\|Concentration]] required), demanding that you commit an evil act. The hand might have a specific act in mind or leave it up to you.  

    **Hand of Vecna Spells**  

    | Spell | Charge Cost |  
    |-------|-------------|  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/finger-of-death-xphb\|Finger of Death]] | 5 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/sleep-xphb\|Sleep]] | 1 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/slow-xphb\|Slow]] | 2 |  
    | [[Rules/Source/spells/teleport-xphb\|Teleport]] | 3 |  
    ^hand-of-vecna-spells  

## Properties of the Eye and Hand

While attuned to both the hand and eye, you gain the following additional benefits:

- **Danger Sense.** You have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/advantage-xphb\|Advantage]] on [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/initiative-xphb\|Initiative]] rolls.  
- **Necrotic Reduction.** As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can target one creature you can see within 5 feet of yourself. The target makes a DC 18 Constitution saving throw, taking `7d6` Necrotic damage on a failed save or half as much damage on a successful one. A creature reduced to 0 [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Points]] by this damage is transformed into [[Rules/Source/traps-hazards/green-slime-xdmg\|green slime]] (see chapter 3) that covers the ground in its space, each 5-foot square of slime representing a separate patch. Nonmagical objects worn or carried by the target that are made of metal or organic material are destroyed by the slime.  
- **Poison Immunity.** You have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/immunity-xphb\|Immunity]] to Poison damage and the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Poisoned\|Poisoned]] condition.  
- **Regeneration.** If you start your turn with at least 1 [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Point]], you regain `1d10` [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Points]].  
- **Wish.** You can cast [[Rules/Source/spells/wish-xphb\|Wish]]. Once used, this property can't be used again until 30 days have passed.  

## Destroying the Eye and Hand

If the [[Rules/Source/items/eye-of-vecna-xdmg\|Eye of Vecna]] and the [[Rules/Source/items/hand-of-vecna-xdmg\|Hand of Vecna]] are both attached to the same creature and that creature is slain by the [[Rules/Source/items/sword-of-kas-xdmg\|Sword of Kas]], both the eye and the hand burst into flame, turn to ash, and are destroyed. Any other attempt to destroy the eye or hand seems to work, but the Artifact reappears in one of Vecna's many hidden vaults, where it waits to be rediscovered.

**Items in this group:**

- [[Rules/Source/items/eye-of-vecna-xdmg\|Eye of Vecna]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/hand-of-vecna-xdmg\|Hand of Vecna]]

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 259*

</div></div>

![/Rules/Source/items/img/eyes-of-charming.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/eyes-of-charming.webp)

*Wondrous item, uncommon*

# Stone of Controlling Earth Elementals
*Wondrous item, rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/stone-of-controlling-earth-elementals.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/stone-of-controlling-earth-elementals.webp)


While touching this 5-pound stone to the ground, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to summon an [[Rules/Source/bestiary/elemental/earth-elemental-xmm\|Earth Elemental]]. The elemental appears in an unoccupied space you choose within 30 feet of yourself, obeys your commands, and takes its turn immediately after you on your [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/initiative-xphb\|Initiative]] count. The elemental disappears after 1 hour, when it dies, or when you dismiss it as a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]]. The stone can't be used this way again until the next dawn.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 312. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

# Flame Tongue
*Rare (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/flame-tongue.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/flame-tongue.webp)


While holding this magic weapon, you can take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] and use a command word to cause flames to engulf the damage-dealing part of the weapon. These flames shed [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bright-light-xphb\|Bright Light]] in a 40-foot radius and [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]] for an additional 40 feet. While the weapon is ablaze, it deals an extra `2d6` Fire damage on a hit. The flames last until you take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] to issue the command again or until you drop, stow, or sheathe the weapon.

> [!embed-item]- Folding Boat
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Folding Boat

</div>



# Folding Boat
*Wondrous item, rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/folding-boat.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/folding-boat.webp)

- **Weight**: 4.0 lbs.

This object appears as a wooden box that measures 12 inches long, 6 inches wide, and 6 inches deep. It weighs 4 pounds and floats. It can be opened to store items inside. This item also has three command words, each requiring a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to use:

- **First Command Word.** The box unfolds into a Rowboat.  
- **Second Command Word.** The box unfolds into a Keelboat.  
- **Third Command Word.** The Folding Boat folds back into a box if no creatures are aboard. Any objects in the vessel that can't fit inside the box remain outside the box as it folds. Any objects in the vessel that can fit inside the box do so.  

When the box becomes a vessel, its weight becomes that of a normal vessel its size, and anything that was stored in the box remains in the boat.

Statistics for the [[Rules/Source/items/rowboat-xphb\|Rowboat]] and [[Rules/Source/items/keelboat-xphb\|Keelboat]] appear in the "Player's Handbook". If either vessel is reduced to 0 [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Points]], the Folding Boat is destroyed.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 263. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

# Frost Brand
*Very rare (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/frost-brand.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/frost-brand.webp)


When you hit with an attack roll using this magic weapon, the target takes an extra `1d6` Cold damage. In addition, while you hold the weapon, you have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/resistance-xphb\|Resistance]] to Fire damage.

In freezing temperatures, the weapon sheds [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bright-light-xphb\|Bright Light]] in a 10-foot radius and [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]] for an additional 10 feet.

When you draw this weapon, you can extinguish all nonmagical flames within 30 feet of yourself. Once used, this property can't be used again for 1 hour.

**Variants**:

*Wondrous item (requires attunement)*

# Gauntlets of Ogre Power

# Gauntlets of Ogre Power
*Wondrous item, uncommon (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/gauntlets-of-ogre-power.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/gauntlets-of-ogre-power.webp)


Your Strength score is 19 while you wear these gauntlets. They have no effect on you if your Strength is 19 or higher without them.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 264. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

> [!embed-item]- Gem of Brightness
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Gem of Brightness

</div>



# Gem of Brightness
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/gem-of-brightness.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/gem-of-brightness.webp)


This prism has 50 charges. While you are holding it, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action and use one of three command words to cause one of the following effects:

- **First Command Word.** The gem sheds [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bright-light-xphb\|Bright Light]] in a 30-foot radius and [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]] for an additional 30 feet. This effect doesn't expend a charge. It lasts until you take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] to repeat the command word or until you use another function of the gem.  
- **Second Command Word.** You expend 1 charge and cause the gem to fire a brilliant beam of light at one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself. The creature must succeed on a DC 15 Constitution saving throw or have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Blinded\|Blinded]] condition for 1 minute. The creature repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.  
- **Third Command Word.** You expend 5 charges and cause the gem to flare with intense light in a 30-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cone-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cone]]. Each creature in the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cone-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cone]] makes a saving throw as if struck by the beam created with the second command word.  

When all of the gem's charges are expended, the gem becomes a nonmagical jewel worth 50 GP.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 264. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Gem of Seeing
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Gem of Seeing

</div>



# Gem of Seeing
*Wondrous item, rare (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/gem-of-seeing.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/gem-of-seeing.webp)


This gem has 3 charges. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can expend 1 charge. For the next 10 minutes, you have [[Rules/Source/senses#Truesight\|Truesight]] out to 120 feet when you peer through the gem.

The gem regains `1d3` expended charges daily at dawn.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 264. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

# Giant Slayer
*Rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/giant-slayer.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/giant-slayer.webp)


You gain a +1 bonus to attack rolls and damage rolls made with this magic weapon.

When you hit a Giant with this weapon, the Giant takes an extra `2d6` damage of the weapon's type and must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Prone\|Prone]] condition.

> [!embed-item]- Glamoured Studded Leather
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Glamoured Studded Leather

</div>



# Glamoured Studded Leather
*Light armor ([[Rules/Source/items/studded-leather-armor-xphb\|studded leather armor]]), rare*  


- **Armor Class**: 12 + Dex modifier
- **Weight**: 13.0 lbs.

While wearing this armor, you gain a +1 bonus to [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/armor-class-xphb\|Armor Class]]. You can also take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] to cause the armor to assume the appearance of a normal set of clothing or some other kind of armor. You decide what it looks like—including color, style, and accessories—but the armor retains its normal bulk and weight. The illusory appearance lasts until you use this property again or doff the armor.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 264. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Gloves of Missile Snaring
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Gloves of Missile Snaring

</div>



# Gloves of Missile Snaring
*Wondrous item, uncommon (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/gloves-of-missile-snaring.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/gloves-of-missile-snaring.webp)


If you're hit by an attack roll made with a Ranged or Thrown weapon while wearing these gloves, you can take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/reaction-xphb\|Reaction]] to reduce the damage by `1d10` plus your Dexterity modifier if you have a free hand. If you reduce the damage to 0, you can catch the ammunition or weapon if it is small enough for you to hold in that hand.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 265. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Gloves of Swimming and Climbing
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Gloves of Swimming and Climbing

</div>



# Gloves of Swimming and Climbing
*Wondrous item, uncommon (requires attunement)*  



While wearing these gloves, climbing and swimming don't cost you extra movement, and you gain a +5 bonus to Strength ([[Rules/Source/skills#Athletics\|Athletics]]) checks made to climb or swim.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 265. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/gloves-of-thievery.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/gloves-of-thievery.webp)

> [!embed-item]- Hag Eye
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Hag Eye

</div>



# Hag Eye
*Wondrous item, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/hag-eye.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/hag-eye.webp)


A Hag Eye has 3 charges. While wearing or holding this item, you can expend 1 charge to cast [[Rules/Source/spells/darkvision-xphb\|Darkvision]] (targeting yourself only) or [[Rules/Source/spells/see-invisibility-xphb\|See Invisibility]]. The Hag Eye regains all expended charges daily at dawn.

## Coven Sensor

The Hag Eye is usually entrusted to a hag's minion for safekeeping and transport. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, a hag who belongs to the coven that created the Hag Eye can see what the Hag Eye sees if the hag and the Hag Eye are on the same plane of existence. This effect lasts as long as the hag maintains [[Rules/Source/conditions#Concentration\|Concentration]]. Multiple hags in the coven can see through the Hag Eye simultaneously.

## Creating a Hag Eye

Only a hag coven can craft this item, which is made from a real eye coated in varnish and often fitted to a pendant or another wearable item. A hag coven can have only one Hag Eye at a time, and creating a new one requires all three members of the coven to perform a special rite. This rite takes 1 hour, and the hags can't perform it if one or more of them has the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Incapacitated\|Incapacitated]] condition. If the hags take any other actions during this rite, the rite fails and ends.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 265*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Hammer of Thunderbolts
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Hammer of Thunderbolts

</div>



# Hammer of Thunderbolts
*Legendary (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/hammer-of-thunderbolts.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/hammer-of-thunderbolts.webp)


You gain a +1 bonus to attack rolls and damage rolls made with this magic weapon.

The weapon has 5 charges. You can expend 1 charge and make a ranged attack with the weapon, hurling it as if it had [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Thrown\|Thrown]] with a normal range of 20 feet and a long range of 60 feet.

If the attack hits, the weapon unleashes a thunderclap audible out to 300 feet. The target and every creature within 30 feet of it other than you must succeed on a DC 17 Constitution saving throw or have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Stunned\|Stunned]] condition until the end of your next turn. Immediately after hitting or missing, the weapon flies back to your hand. The weapon regains `1d4 + 1` expended charges daily at dawn.

## Giant's Bane

While you are attuned to the weapon and wearing either a [[Rules/Source/items/belt-of-giant-strength-xdmg\|Belt of Giant Strength]] or [[Rules/Source/items/gauntlets-of-ogre-power-xdmg\|Gauntlets of Ogre Power]] to which you are also attuned, you gain the following benefits:

- **Giants' Bane.** When you roll a 20 on the `d20` for an attack roll made with this weapon against a Giant, the creature must succeed on a DC 17 Constitution saving throw or die.  
- **Might of Giants.** The Strength score bestowed by your [[Rules/Source/items/belt-of-giant-strength-xdmg\|Belt of Giant Strength]] or [[Rules/Source/items/gauntlets-of-ogre-power-xdmg\|Gauntlets of Ogre Power]] increases by 4, to a maximum of 30.  

**Variants**:
- [Maul of Thunderbolts](#Maul%20of%20Thunderbolts)
- [Warhammer of Thunderbolts](#Warhammer%20of%20Thunderbolts)

### Maul of Thunderbolts

- **Damage**: 2d6 bludgeoning
- **Properties**: [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Heavy\|Heavy]], [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Two-Handed\|Two-Handed]]
- **Weight**: 10.0 lbs.

### Warhammer of Thunderbolts

- **Damage**:
  - One-handed: 1d8 bludgeoning
  - Two-handed: 1d10 bludgeoning
- **Properties**: [[Rules/Source/item-properties#Versatile\|Versatile]]
- **Weight**: 5.0 lbs.


*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 265. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

> [!embed-item]- Hat of Many Spells
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Hat of Many Spells

</div>



# Hat of Many Spells
*Wondrous item, spellcasting focus, very rare (requires attunement by a wizard)*  



This pointed hat has the following properties.

## Spellcasting Focus

While holding the hat, you can use it as a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/spellcasting-focus-xphb\|Spellcasting Focus]] for your Wizard spells. Any spell you cast using the hat gains a special Somatic component: you must reach into the hat and "pull" the spell out of it.

## Unknown Spell

While holding the hat, you can try to cast a level 1+ spell you don't know. The spell must be on the Wizard spell list, it must be of a level you can cast, and it can't have Material components costing more than 1,000 GP. Once you decide on the spell, you must expend a spell slot of the spell's level. Then, to determine whether you cast the spell, make an Intelligence ([[Rules/Source/skills#Arcana\|Arcana]]) check (DC 10 plus the spell's level). On a successful check, you cast the spell using its normal casting time, and you can't use this property again until you finish a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/short-rest-xphb\|Short]] or [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/long-rest-xphb\|Long Rest]]. On a failed check, you fail to cast the spell and a random effect occurs instead, determined by rolling on the following table.

Any spell you cast from the hat uses your spell save DC and spell attack bonus.

| dice: 1d100 | Effect |
|-------------|--------|
| 01-50 | You cast a random spell determined by rolling `1d10`: on a **1**, [[Rules/Source/spells/enlarge-reduce-xphb\|Enlarge/Reduce]] (enlarge effect); on a **2**, [[Rules/Source/spells/enlarge-reduce-xphb\|Enlarge/Reduce]] (reduce effect); on a **3**, [[Rules/Source/spells/faerie-fire-xphb\|Faerie Fire]]; on a **4**, [[Rules/Source/spells/fireball-xphb\|Fireball]]; on a **5**, [[Rules/Source/spells/gust-of-wind-xphb\|Gust of Wind]]; on a **6**, [[Rules/Source/spells/invisibility-xphb\|Invisibility]] (cast on yourself); on a **7**, [[Rules/Source/spells/lightning-bolt-xphb\|Lightning Bolt]]; on an **8**, [[Rules/Source/spells/phantasmal-force-xphb\|Phantasmal Force]]; on a **9**, [[Rules/Source/spells/polymorph-xphb\|Polymorph]]; on a **10**, [[Rules/Source/spells/stinking-cloud-xphb\|Stinking Cloud]]. |
| 51-55 | You have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Stunned\|Stunned]] condition until the end of your next turn, believing something awesome just happened. |
| 56-60 | A harmless swarm of butterflies fills a 10-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cube-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cube]] within 30 feet of yourself. The swarm disperses after 1 minute. |
| 61-65 | You pull a nonmagical object out of the hat. Roll `1d4` to determine the object: on a **1**, a vial of Acid; on a **2**, a flask of Alchemist's Fire; on a **3**, a Crowbar; on a **4**, a lit Torch. |
| 66-70 | You suffer a bout of "magic sickness" and have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Poisoned\|Poisoned]] condition for 1 hour. |
| 71-75 | You have the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Petrified\|Petrified]] condition until the end of your next turn. |
| 76-80 | You pull a nonmagical object out of the hat. Roll `1d4` to determine the object: on a **1**, a [[Rules/Source/items/dagger-xphb\|Dagger]]; on a **2**, a [[Rules/Source/items/rope-xphb\|Rope]] with a [[Rules/Source/items/grappling-hook-xphb\|Grappling Hook]] tied to one end; on a **3**, a bag of [[Rules/Source/items/caltrops-xphb\|Caltrops]]; on a **4**, a gem worth 50 GP. |
| 81-85 | A creature appears in an unoccupied space as close to you as possible. The creature isn't under your control and acts as it normally would, and it disappears after 1 hour or when it drops to 0 [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Points]]. Roll `1d4` to determine the creature: on a **1**, a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/camel-xmm\|Camel]]; on a **2**, a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/constrictor-snake-xmm\|Constrictor Snake]]; on a **3**, an [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/elephant-xmm\|Elephant]]; on a **4**, a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/mule-xmm\|Mule]]. |
| 86-90 | A [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hostile-attitude-xphb\|Hostile]] [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/swarm-of-bats-xmm\|Swarm of Bats]] flies out of the hat, occupies your space, and attacks you. |
| 91-95 | A vertical, 10-foot-diameter, two-way portal to another plane of existence opens in an unoccupied space within 30 feet of you and remains open until the end of your next turn. The DM determines where it leads. |
| 96-00 | You pull a magic item out of the hat. Roll `1d6` to determine the item's rarity: on a **1-3**, Common; on a **4-5**, Uncommon; on a **6**, Rare. The DM chooses the item, which disappears after 1 hour if it's not consumed or destroyed before then. |
*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 266. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

# Hat of Vermin

![/Rules/Source/items/img/hat-of-vermin.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/hat-of-vermin.webp)


This hat has 3 charges. While holding the hat, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to expend 1 charge and summon your choice of a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/bat-xmm\|Bat]], a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/frog-xmm\|Frog]], or a [[Rules/Source/bestiary/beast/rat-xmm\|Rat]]. The summoned creature magically appears in the hat and tries to get away from you as quickly as possible. The hat regains all expended charges daily at dawn.

This cone-shaped hat is adorned with moons and stars. While you are wearing it, you gain the following benefits.

## Spellcasting Focus

You can use the hat as a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/spellcasting-focus-xphb\|Spellcasting Focus]] for your Wizard spells.

## Unknown Spell

As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can try to cast a cantrip that you don't know. > [!embed-item]- Helm of Brilliance
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Helm of Brilliance

</div>



# Helm of Brilliance
*Wondrous item, very rare (requires attunement)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/helm-of-brilliance.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/helm-of-brilliance.webp)


This helm is set with `1d10` diamonds, `2d10` rubies, `3d10` fire opals, and `4d10` opals. Any gem pried from the helm crumbles to dust. When all the gems are removed or destroyed, the helm loses its magic.

You gain the following benefits while wearing the helm.

## Diamond Light

As long as it has at least one diamond, the helm emits a 30-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]]. When at least one Undead is within that area, the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] is filled with [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]]. Any Undead that starts its turn in that area takes `1d6` Radiant damage.

## Fire Opal Flames

As long as the helm has at least one fire opal, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to cause one weapon you are holding to burst into flames. The flames emit [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bright-light-xphb\|Bright Light]] in a 10-foot radius and [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/dim-light-xphb\|Dim Light]] for an additional 10 feet. The flames are harmless to you and the weapon. When you hit with an attack using the blazing weapon, the target takes an extra `1d6` Fire damage. The flames last until you take a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] to extinguish them or until you drop or stow the weapon.

## Ruby Resistance

As long as the helm has at least one ruby, you have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/resistance-xphb\|Resistance]] to Fire damage.

## Spells

You can cast one of the following spells (save DC 18), using one of the helm's gems of the specified type as a component: [[Rules/Source/spells/daylight-xphb\|Daylight]] (opal), [[Rules/Source/spells/fireball-xphb\|Fireball]] (fire opal), [[Rules/Source/spells/prismatic-spray-xphb\|Prismatic Spray]] (diamond), or [[Rules/Source/spells/wall-of-fire-xphb\|Wall of Fire]] (ruby). The gem is destroyed when the spell is cast and disappears from the helm.

## Taking Fire Damage

Roll `1d20` if you are wearing the helm and take Fire damage as a result of failing a saving throw against a spell. On a roll of 1, the helm emits beams of light from its remaining gems and is then destroyed. Each creature within a 60-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from you must succeed on a DC 17 Dexterity saving throw or be struck by a beam, taking Radiant damage equal to the number of gems in the helm.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 268. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

> [!embed-item]- Heward's Handy Haversack

<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

# Heward's Handy Haversack

# Heward's Handy Haversack
*Wondrous item, rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/hewards-handy-haversack.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/hewards-handy-haversack.webp)

- **Weight**: 5.0 lbs.

This backpack has a central pouch and two side pouches, each of which is an extradimensional space. Each side pouch can hold up to 200 pounds of material, not exceeding a volume of 25 cubic feet. The central pouch can hold up to 500 pounds of material, not exceeding a volume of 64 cubic feet. The haversack always weighs 5 pounds, regardless of its contents.

Retrieving an item from the haversack requires a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action or a [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/bonus-action-xphb\|Bonus Action]] (your choice). When you reach into the haversack for a specific item, the item is always magically on top.

If any of its pouches is overloaded, pierced, or torn, the haversack ruptures and is destroyed. If the haversack is destroyed, its contents are lost forever, although an Artifact always turns up again somewhere. If the haversack is turned inside out, its contents spill forth unharmed, and the haversack must be put right before it can be used again.

Each pouch of the haversack holds enough air for 10 minutes of breathing, divided by the number of breathing creatures inside.

Placing the haversack inside an extradimensional space created by a [[Rules/Source/items/bag-of-holding-xdmg\|Bag of Holding]], [[Rules/Source/items/portable-hole-xdmg\|Portable Hole]], or similar item instantly destroys both items and opens a gate to the Astral Plane. The gate originates where the one item was placed inside the other. Any creature within 10 feet of the gate and not behind [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cover-xphb\|Total Cover]] is sucked through it and deposited in a random location on the Astral Plane. The gate then closes. The gate is one-way only and can't be reopened.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 269. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> (as) and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>

A pinch is enough to season a single meal. The pouch regains `1d6 + 4` expended charges daily at dawn.

# Holy Avenger
*Legendary (requires attunement by a paladin)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/holy-avenger.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/holy-avenger.webp)


You gain a +3 bonus to attack rolls and damage rolls made with this magic weapon. When you hit a Fiend or an Undead with it, that creature takes an extra `2d10` Radiant damage.

While you hold the drawn weapon, it creates a 10-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from you. You and all creatures [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/friendly-attitude-xphb\|Friendly]] to you in the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/advantage-xphb\|Advantage]] on saving throws against spells and other magical effects. If you have 17 or more levels in the Paladin class, the size of the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] increases to 30 feet.

**Variants**:
- [Holy Avenger Antimatter Rifle](#Holy%20Avenger%20Antimatter%20Rifle)
- [Holy Avenger Automatic Rifle](#Holy%20Avenger%20Automatic%20Rifle)
- [Holy Avenger Battleaxe](#Holy%20Avenger%20Battleaxe)
- [Holy Avenger Blowgun](#Holy%20Avenger%20Blowgun)
- [Holy Avenger Club](#Holy%20Avenger%20Club)
- [Holy Avenger Dagger](#Holy%20Avenger%20Dagger)
- [Holy Avenger Dart](#Holy%20Avenger%20Dart)
- [Holy Avenger Double-Bladed Scimitar](#Holy%20Avenger%20Double-Bladed%20Scimitar)
- [Holy Avenger Flail](#Holy%20Avenger%20Flail)
- [Holy Avenger Glaive](#Holy%20Avenger%20Glaive)

> [!embed-item]- Horn of Blasting
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Horn of Blasting

</div>



# Horn of Blasting
*Wondrous item, instrument ([[Rules/Source/items/horn-xphb\|horn]]), rare*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/horn-of-blasting.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/horn-of-blasting.webp)


You can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to blow the horn, which emits a thunderous blast in a 30-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cone-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cone]] that is audible out to 600 feet. Each creature in the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cone-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cone]] makes a DC 15 Constitution saving throw. On a failed save, a creature takes `5d8` Thunder damage and has the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Deafened\|Deafened]] condition for 1 minute. On a successful save, a creature takes half as much damage only. Glass or crystal objects in the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/cone-area-of-effect-xphb\|Cone]] that aren't being worn or carried take `10d8` Thunder damage.

Each use of the horn's magic has a 20 percent chance of causing the horn to explode. The explosion deals `10d6` Force damage to the user and destroys the horn.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Horn of Silent Alarm
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Horn of Silent Alarm

</div>



# Horn of Silent Alarm
*Wondrous item, instrument ([[Rules/Source/items/horn-xphb\|horn]]), common*  



This horn has 4 charges and regains `1d4` expended charges daily at dawn. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can blow the horn while expending 1 charge. One creature of your choice hears the horn's blare, provided that creature is within 600 feet of the horn. No other creature hears the horn.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Horn of Valhalla
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Horn of Valhalla

</div>



# Horn of Valhalla
*Wondrous item*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/horn-of-valhalla.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/horn-of-valhalla.webp)


You can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to blow this horn. In response, warrior spirits from the plane of Ysgard appear in unoccupied spaces within 60 feet of you. Each spirit uses the [[Rules/Source/bestiary/humanoid/berserker-xmm\|Berserker]] stat block and returns to Ysgard after 1 hour or when it drops to 0 [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/hit-points-xphb\|Hit Points]]. The spirits look like living, breathing warriors, and they have [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/immunity-xphb\|Immunity]] to the [[Rules/Source/conditions#Charmed\|Charmed]] and [[Rules/Source/conditions#Frightened\|Frightened]] conditions. Once you use the horn, it can't be used again until 7 days have passed.

Four types of Horn of Valhalla are known to exist, each made of a different metal. The horn's type determines how many spirits it summons, as well as the requirement for its use. The DM chooses the horn's type or determines it randomly by rolling on the following table. If you blow the horn without meeting its requirement, the summoned spirits attack you.

If you meet the requirement, they are [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/friendly-attitude-xphb\|Friendly]] to you and your allies and follow your commands.

| dice: 1d100 | Horn Type | Spirits | Requirement |
|-------------|-----------|---------|-------------|
| 01-40 | [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-silver-xdmg\|Silver]] | 2 | None |
| 41-75 | [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-brass-xdmg\|Brass]] | 3 | [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/proficiency-xphb\|Proficiency]] with all Simple weapons |
| 76-90 | [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-bronze-xdmg\|Bronze]] | 4 | Training with all Medium armor |
| 91-00 | [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-iron-xdmg\|Iron]] | 5 | [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/proficiency-xphb\|Proficiency]] with all Martial weapons |

**Items in this group:**

- [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-brass-xdmg\|Horn of Valhalla, Brass]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-bronze-xdmg\|Horn of Valhalla, Bronze]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-iron-xdmg\|Horn of Valhalla, Iron]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/horn-of-valhalla-silver-xdmg\|Horn of Valhalla, Silver]]

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Horseshoes of a Zephyr
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Horseshoes of a Zephyr

</div>



# Horseshoes of a Zephyr
*Wondrous item, very rare*  



These horseshoes come in a set of four. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can touch one of the horseshoes to the hoof of a horse or similar creature, whereupon the horseshoe affixes itself to the hoof. Removing a horseshoe also takes a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action.

While all four shoes are affixed to the hooves of a horse or similar creature, they allow the creature to move normally while floating 4 inches above a surface. This effect means the creature can cross or stand above nonsolid or unstable surfaces, such as water or lava. The creature leaves no tracks and ignores [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/difficult-terrain-xphb\|Difficult Terrain]]. In addition, the creature can travel for up to 12 hours a day without gaining [[Rules/Source/conditions#Exhaustion\|Exhaustion]] levels from extended travel.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Horseshoes of Speed
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Horseshoes of Speed

</div>



# Horseshoes of Speed
*Wondrous item, rare*  



These horseshoes come in a set of four. As a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action, you can touch one of the horseshoes to the hoof of a horse or similar creature, whereupon the horseshoe affixes itself to the hoof. Removing a horseshoe also takes a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action.

While all four horseshoes are attached to the same creature, its [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/speed-xphb\|Speed]] is increased by 30 feet.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


### I

> [!embed-item]- Immovable Rod
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Immovable Rod

</div>



# Immovable Rod
*Rod, uncommon*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/immovable-rod.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/immovable-rod.webp)

- **Weight**: 2.0 lbs.

This iron rod has a button on one end. You can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to press the button, which causes the rod to become magically fixed in place. Until you or another creature takes a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to push the button again, the rod doesn't move, even if it defies gravity. The rod can hold up to 8,000 pounds of weight. More weight causes the rod to deactivate and fall. A creature can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Utilize\|Utilize]] action to make a DC 30 Strength ([[Rules/Source/skills#Athletics\|Athletics]]) check, moving the fixed rod up to 10 feet on a successful check.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 270. Available in the <span title='Systems Reference Document (5.2)'>SRD</span> and the Free Rules (2024)*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Instrument of Illusions
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Instrument of Illusions

</div>



# Instrument of Illusions
*Wondrous item, common*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/instrument-of-illusions.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/instrument-of-illusions.webp)


While you are playing this [[Rules/Source/items/musical-instrument-xphb\|musical instrument]], you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to create harmless, illusory visual effects within a 5-foot [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] originating from the instrument. If you are a Bard, the size of the [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/emanation-area-of-effect-xphb\|Emanation]] increases to 15 feet. Sample visual effects include luminous musical notes, a spectral dancer, butterflies, and gently falling snow. The magical effects have neither substance nor sound, and they are obviously illusory. The effects end when you stop playing.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 271*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Instrument of Scribing
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Instrument of Scribing

</div>



# Instrument of Scribing
*Wondrous item, common*  



This [[Rules/Source/items/musical-instrument-xphb\|musical instrument]] has 3 charges and regains all expended charges daily at dawn. While you are playing it, you can take a [[Rules/Source/actions#Magic\|Magic]] action to expend 1 charge and write a magical message on a nonmagical object or surface that you can see within 30 feet of yourself. The message can be up to six words long and is written in a language you know. If you are a Bard, you can scribe an additional seven words and make the message glow faintly, allowing it to be seen in nonmagical [[Rules/Source/variant-rules/darkness-xphb\|Darkness]]. Casting the [[Rules/Source/spells/dispel-magic-xphb\|Dispel Magic]] spell on the message erases it. Otherwise, the message fades away after 24 hours.

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 271*

</div></div>


> [!embed-item]- Instrument of the Bards
> 
<div class="transclusion internal-embed is-loaded"><div class="markdown-embed">

<div class="markdown-embed-title">

# Instrument of the Bards

</div>



# Instrument of the Bards
*Wondrous item (requires attunement by a bard)*  
![/Rules/Source/items/img/instrument-of-the-bards.webp\|right wmed](/img/user/Rules/Source/items/img/instrument-of-the-bards.webp)


An Instrument of the Bards is superior to an ordinary instrument in every way. Seven types of these instruments exist, each named after a bard college. A creature that attempts to play the instrument without being attuned to it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or take `2d4` Psychic damage.

You can play the instrument to cast one of its spells. Once the instrument has been used to cast a spell, it can't be used to cast that spell again until the next dawn. The spells use your spellcasting ability and spell save DC.

All Instrument of the Bards can be used to cast the following spells: [[Rules/Source/spells/fly-xphb\|Fly]], [[Rules/Source/spells/invisibility-xphb\|Invisibility]], [[Rules/Source/spells/levitate-xphb\|Levitate]], [[Rules/Source/spells/protection-from-evil-and-good-xphb\|Protection from Evil and Good]]

**Items in this group:**

- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-anstruth-harp-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Anstruth Harp]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-canaith-mandolin-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Canaith Mandolin]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-cli-lyre-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Cli Lyre]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-doss-lute-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Doss Lute]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-fochlucan-bandore-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Fochlucan Bandore]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-mac-fuirmidh-cittern-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Mac-Fuirmidh Cittern]]
- [[Rules/Source/items/instrument-of-the-bards-ollamh-harp-xdmg\|Instrument of the Bards, Ollamh Harp]]

*Source: Dungeon Master's Guide (2024) p. 272*
